## Best date plans

### 2026-09-13 — Smorgasburg Matsuri: Japan Meets Brooklyn + Haenyeo

**Smorgasburg Matsuri: Japan Meets Brooklyn, then a meal.** Korean at Haenyeo. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=239+Fifth+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/smorgasburg-matsuri-japan-meets-brooklyn/) · [Restaurant](https://haenyeobk.com/)

## Where to eat

Verified restaurant-owned menus where available and kept heatmap leads only when the dated source supports them; older April/May openings are either reclassified as restaurants or rejected when duplicated by stronger official evidence.

Dining research: 2026-09-13T10:31:31+00:00

No kept item relies on an unsourced Michelin-star claim. Checked sources mention no candidate-specific Michelin star or current Michelin Guide selection requiring retention; Eater's Michelin-starred taqueria note concerned El Califa de León, which was not one of the candidates. Some official sites returned only page shells via live fetch, so captured public HTML/JSON-LD evidence was used where clearer.

### Weekday aperitivo window at Ci Siamo

Special menus — Ci Siamo

Restaurant-owned pages verify a weekday aperitivo service in the Bar & Lounge, useful for an earlier, less formal Italian stop before dinner service.

Price: Aperitivo menu examples include Cast Iron Focaccia $18, Gnocco Fritto $16, Mortadella Con Pistacchio $14, Pizza Bianca $15 and Pizzette $16; lunch/dinner menu examples include tagliatelle $25 and Chicken Milanese $29. Sources: https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/ and https://www.cisiamo.com/location/ci-siamo/  
Schedule: Aperitivo in the Bar & Lounge Monday-Friday 3:00pm-4:30pm; lunch daily 11:30am-3:00pm; dinner daily 4:30pm-10:00pm. Source: https://www.cisiamo.com/location/ci-siamo/  
Conditions: Reservations link is available; whether aperitivo requires or accepts reservations is not stated in checked text. Tax, tip and service charges unknown.  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Manhatta three- or four-course dinner prix fixe

Special menus — Manhatta

The official menu lists a high-view fixed-price dinner format with seasonal choices such as tomato carpaccio, bluefin tuna, soft-shell crab, ribeye, duck and desserts.
